\subsection*{algorithm 5 ocr}
\begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced,breakable,colback=white,colframe=Muted!45,boxrule=0.45pt,arc=1.5pt,left=6pt,right=6pt,top=5pt,bottom=5pt,title={\texttt{A5-CHAINS-13}},title after break={\texttt{A5-CHAINS-13}\ (continued)},fonttitle=\headingfont\bfseries\color{Navy},coltitle=Navy]
